from psycopg2.extras import RealDictCursor
refs=['ORD-0cc7d68012e750cb8b','ORD-2a8724aac5ef54ccb4','ORD-cd6bd9894d3e5cbcb1','ORD-7d3714c13e565a29ac','ORD-32491988dcec5c7e83','ORD-c50d9a0414f75dd797','ORD-f703c4d2f16359b29b','ORD-b2d0e86c90a659bcb9','ORD-fe9e62b1b8da564898','ORD-3a071c95ad665fd380','ORD-3438b3dc7c2a57e280','ORD-277c1d41487d5549b6']

python3 - <<'PY'
import json,urllib.request
import psycopg2
from psycopg2.extras import RealDictCursor
conn=psycopg2.connect(dbname='commerce',user='commerce',host='127.0.0.1',port=5433)
cur=conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
cur.execute("SELECT request_id,body,response FROM requests WHERE request_id=%s",('93ad4614-6c4d-59fa-a9a8-63d2fb117463',))
row=cur.fetchone(); conn.close()
request=urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8080/commands',data=json.dumps(row['body']).encode(),headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'},method='POST')
with urllib.request.urlopen(request) as response:
    status=response.status; actual=json.load(response)
print('wal_recovered_retry_status',status,'stored_response_matches',actual==row['response'])
if status!=200 or actual!=row['response']: raise SystemExit(1)

set -o pipefail
set -euo pipefail
backup=$(ls -td /var/backups/commerce-incident-*-phase2 | head -1)

runuser -u postgres -- /usr/lib/postgresql/17/bin/pg_ctl -D /tmp/commerce-recovery -m fast -w stop
rm -rf /tmp/commerce-recovery /tmp/commerce-recovery-wal /tmp/commerce-recovery.log /tmp/carrier-bookings.json /tmp/commerce-connector-config.json /tmp/dispatch.patch
stamp=$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)
final=/var/backups/commerce-incident-$stamp-final
mkdir -p "$final"
pg_dump -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5433 -U commerce -d commerce -Fc -f "$final/commerce-final.dump"
cp -a /srv/commerce/service.py "$final/service.py"
cp -a /var/lib/commerce-dispatch.sqlite "$final/commerce-dispatch.sqlite"
curl -sS 'http://127.0.0.1:8099/bookings' -o "$final/carrier-bookings.json"
curl -sS 'http://127.0.0.1:8083/connectors/commerce-outbox/config' -o "$final/connect-config.json"
cat > "$final/VALIDATION.txt" <<'EOF'
Commerce incident recovery validation
- Every API 201 command in api.log is present in requests.
- requests and outbox counts match with no unmatched event IDs.
- All accepted release events have exactly one matching carrier booking and receipt.
- No cancelled or draft order has a release event/booking.
- Every order matches its latest accepted outbox event.
- Old-primary requests and outbox rows match PITR recovery exactly.
- Restored order GET responses and stored request retries match.
- Supervisor services, API health, carrier health, connector task, replication, and consumer lag were verified.
EOF
sha256sum "$final"/* > "$final/SHA256SUMS"
printf '%s\n' "final_backup=$final"
ls -lh "$final"
printf '%s\n' '--- isolated recovery cleanup ---'
ss -lntp | grep ':55432' || true
ps auxww | grep '[p]ostgres.*55432' || true
printf '%s\n' '--- production status ---'
supervisorctl status
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8080/health; echo
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8099/health; echo
printf '%s\n' '--- connector ---'
curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:8083/connectors/commerce-outbox/status | python3 -m json.tool
printf '%s\n' '--- replication ---'
PGPASSWORD=postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5433 -U postgres -d postgres -X -P pager=off -c "SELECT application_name,state,sync_state,sent_lsn,write_lsn,flush_lsn,replay_lsn FROM pg_stat_replication;"
PGPASSWORD=postgres psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5433 -U postgres -d postgres -X -P pager=off -c "SELECT slot_name,plugin,slot_type,active,restart_lsn,confirmed_flush_lsn FROM pg_replication_slots WHERE slot_name='commerce_outbox';"
printf '%s\n' '--- consumer lag ---'
/opt/kafka/bin/kafka-consumer-groups.sh --bootstrap-server 127.0.0.1:9092 --describe --group commerce-dispatch
printf '%s\n' '--- post-restart errors ---'
awk '$1=="2026-09-24" && $2>="04:31:23" {print}' /var/log/commerce/api.log /var/log/commerce/dispatch.log | grep -E 'ERROR|Traceback|command failed|carrier rejected|temporarily unavailable' || true
printf '%s\n' '--- patched code lines ---'
nl -ba /srv/commerce/service.py | sed -n '162,205p'

   162	def dispatch():
   163	    from kafka import KafkaConsumer
   164	    settings = config()
   165	    ledger = sqlite3.connect('/var/lib/commerce-dispatch.sqlite')
   166	    ledger.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS receipts(source_id integer PRIMARY KEY,event_id text NOT NULL,reference text NOT NULL,booking_id text NOT NULL)')
   167	    ledger.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS receipts_v2(event_id text PRIMARY KEY,source_id integer NOT NULL,reference text NOT NULL,booking_id text NOT NULL)')
   168	    ledger.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO receipts_v2(event_id,source_id,reference,booking_id) '
   169	                   'SELECT event_id,source_id,reference,booking_id FROM receipts')
   170	    ledger.commit()
   171	    consumer = KafkaConsumer(settings['topic'],bootstrap_servers='127.0.0.1:9092',
   172	                             group_id=settings['group'],enable_auto_commit=False,
   173	                             auto_offset_reset='earliest',max_poll_records=1,
   174	                             value_deserializer=lambda raw:json.loads(raw) if raw else None)
   175	    for message in consumer:
   176	        value = message.value
   177	        event = value.get('after') if value else None
   178	        if not event or event.get('kind') != 'release':
   179	            consumer.commit()
   180	            continue
   181	        # The event UUID is stable across database failovers and sequence collisions.
   182	        old = ledger.execute('SELECT booking_id FROM receipts_v2 WHERE event_id=?',(event['event_id'],)).fetchone()
   183	        if old:
   184	            logging.info('already dispatched event %s: %s',event['event_id'],old[0])
   185	            consumer.commit()
   186	            continue
   187	        payload = event['payload']
   188	        if isinstance(payload,str):
   189	            payload = json.loads(payload)
   190	        body = {k:payload[k] for k in ('customer','address','items')}
   191	        body.update(client_reference=event['event_id'],order_reference=event['reference'])
   192	        while True:
   193	            try:
   194	                status,receipt = http(settings['carrier']+'/bookings',body,{'Idempotency-Key':'event:'+str(event['event_id'])})
   195	                if status in (200,201):
   196	                    break
   197	                logging.error('carrier rejected outbox %s: HTTP %s %s',event['id'],status,receipt)
   198	            except (OSError,ValueError):
   199	                logging.exception('carrier request failed for outbox %s',event['id'])
   200	            time.sleep(5)
   201	        with ledger:
   202	            ledger.execute('INSERT INTO receipts_v2(event_id,source_id,reference,booking_id) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',
   203	                           (event['event_id'],event['id'],event['reference'],receipt['booking_id']))
   204	        consumer.commit()
   205	        logging.info('dispatched %s (%s): %s',event['reference'],event['event_id'],receipt['booking_id'])

set -euo pipefail
python3 - <<'PY'
import json,re,sqlite3,urllib.request
import psycopg2
from psycopg2.extras import RealDictCursor
log=open('/var/log/commerce/api.log').read()
accepted=set(re.findall(r'command ([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}) .* -> 201',log))
conn=psycopg2.connect(dbname='commerce',user='commerce',host='127.0.0.1',port=5433)
cur=conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor)
cur.execute('SELECT request_id FROM requests'); stored={r['request_id'] for r in cur.fetchall()}
cur.execute("SELECT event_id,reference,payload FROM outbox WHERE kind='release'")
releases=cur.fetchall(); release_ids={r['event_id'] for r in releases}
cur.execute('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM orders'); orders=cur.fetchone()['n']
cur.execute('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM requests'); requests=cur.fetchone()['n']
cur.execute('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM outbox'); outbox=cur.fetchone()['n']
conn.close()
with urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8099/bookings') as response:
    bookings=json.load(response).get('bookings',[])
booking_ids={b.get('client_reference') for b in bookings}
ledger=sqlite3.connect('/var/lib/commerce-dispatch.sqlite')
receipt_ids={r[0] for r in ledger.execute('SELECT event_id FROM receipts_v2').fetchall()}
ledger.close()
errors=[]
if accepted-stored: errors.append(f'{len(accepted-stored)} accepted requests missing')
if requests!=outbox: errors.append('request/outbox count mismatch')
if release_ids!=booking_ids: errors.append('release/booking coverage mismatch')
if release_ids!=receipt_ids: errors.append('release/receipt coverage mismatch')
if len(bookings)!=len(booking_ids): errors.append('duplicate carrier bookings')
print('accepted_201',len(accepted),'stored_requests',len(stored),'orders',orders,'requests',requests,'outbox',outbox,'releases',len(releases),'bookings',len(bookings),'receipts',len(receipt_ids))
print('errors',len(errors),errors)
if errors: raise SystemExit(1)
